36.08%，公司主要生產產品為低密度聚乙

主要客戶：

葉家宏 yili.chen@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

亞聚 (1308 TT) 
EVA 新增產能壓力仍大

永豐觀點 
隨著美伊戰爭情緒有所緩解，原油價格回落，塑化產品報價反轉，終端客戶備貨心
態趨於保守， 短期亞聚雖有望受惠低價庫存利益，長期則仍須面對中國大量投入的
新增產能壓力，壓抑利差表現，故研究部維持保守看待亞聚獲利表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持中立： 近期因美伊戰爭情緒有所緩解，終端客戶備貨已趨於保守觀
望，EVA 報價反轉下跌，原料乙烯、VAM 仍位居高檔，將造成 EVA 利差持續受
限，而中國2H26 預期仍有超過 100 萬噸 EVA 新增產能將投產，EVA 供給壓力預
期將持續增大，壓抑 EVA 產品利差，故研究部維持亞聚中立投資建議。

ESG 評析 
亞聚企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

亞聚為 LDPE 及 EVA 生產廠商： 亞聚為台聚集團旗下子公司，台聚間接持股
36.08%，公司主要生產產品為低密度聚乙烯樹脂（LDPE Resin）及乙烯醋酸乙烯
酯共聚合樹脂（EVA Resin），LDPE 產品涵蓋淋膜級、射出級及薄膜級，EVA 產品
則有高端發泡級、淋膜級、太陽能級及電纜線級，亞聚 LDPE 與 EVA 合計總產能
為 15 萬噸，共 4 套高壓釜式生產線，1 套生產 LDPE，其餘 3 套生產 EVA，EVA
依照醋酸乙烯（ VA）含量分成不同級別產品， 2025 營收占比為：太陽能級 PV
（15%） 、塗覆級Lami（43%） 、通用發泡級Foam（L）（21%） 、高VA 含量發泡
級 Foam（H）（21%）。 
1Q26 稅後 EPS 為-0.2 元 ， 低於預期 ：亞聚 1Q26 營收 13.02 億元 （-7.2% QoQ，
-15.2% YoY） ，低於預期，主因3M26 美伊戰爭爆發，客戶積極追料，然漲價多反
應於 4M25 之後的訂單，致使 1Q26 營收未如預期，毛利率 5.66%（-0.86 pts. 
QoQ，-0.96 pts. YoY ） ，低於預期，主要係因原料乙烯於3M25 報價急漲，然亞
聚漲價訂單多反應於 4M25 之後，影響 1Q26 毛利率未如預期，稅後虧損 1.2 億
元 （虧損縮小QoQ，虧損縮小 YoY） ，其中，業外轉投資古雷石化認列一次性地方
政府補助款，認列古雷石化虧損金額較預期低，1Q26 稅後 EPS -0.2 元，低於預
期。 
2Q26 獲利預期承壓：展望 2Q26，中國自 4M26 開始取消光伏出口退稅 13%，
帶動 EVA 行情於 1Q26 止跌反彈，惟市場買氣在漲價後受到壓抑，光伏EVA 需求
明顯轉弱，而市場行情受到美伊戰爭預期緩解，且中國 EVA 供過於求陰影壟罩影
響 ， 下游客戶觀望氣氛濃厚 ， 備貨趨於保守 ， 而亞聚受到中油新三輕設備異常影響 ，
於 4M26 至 5M26 開工率預期降至 50 至 60%，故預期營收將低於 1Q26，而受
惠高價訂單出貨及低價庫存利益 ，本業獲利預期好轉， 惟缺少業外古雷石化一次性
補助款，認列虧損金額應將較 1Q26 擴大，壓抑亞聚獲利表現，基此，研究部預估
2Q26 營收 11.27 億元 （-13.5％QoQ，-16.0％ YoY） ，預估毛利率8.23%（+2.57 
pts. QoQ，+4.75 pts. YoY） ，預估稅後虧損3.2 億元（虧損擴大 QoQ，虧損擴大 
YoY） ，稅後EPS -0.54 元。

### Page 3

3

EVA 新增產能壓力仍大，維持中立投資建議：展望後市，隨著美伊戰爭情緒有所
緩解，下游客戶擔心行情反轉，備貨心態趨於保守，造成 EVA 價格持續走跌，然
面對原料乙烯、VAM 價格仍位居高檔，EVA 利差將再次受限，而觀察中國 2H26
仍有超過 100 萬噸的 EVA 產能將投產，EVA 新增產能壓力持續增大，將持續壓抑
EVA 利差表現，故研究部對亞聚獲利表現維持保守看待，預估2026 年營收 55.11
億元（-4.1％ YoY），毛利率 4.02%，稅後虧損 9.28 億元（虧損縮小 YoY），稅
後 EPS -1.56 元，維持中立投資建議。

2Q26 價格漲幅逾五成

維持買進評等，目標價上調至 183 元，潛在上漲空間 27% 
1. 1Q26 財報優於預期，毛利率 53.4%、EPS 2.25 元。 
2. 記憶體價格幾確定將上漲至年底，2H 合併毛利率預料挑戰 70%大關。 
5. 上修今明年獲利預估，適用評價上調至 FY27 PBR 5.0X，ROE 估約逾 60%。

1Q26 財報優於預期，毛利率 53.4%、EPS 2.25 元 
華邦電 1Q26 合併營收 382.53 億元，QoQ+43.7%，YoY+91.3%，數字優於富邦
預期。其中，DRAM 占比 46.6%，QoQ +93%；Flash 占比 31.6%，QoQ+22%； 
Logic 占 20.6%，QoQ+13%。價格方面，1Q26 CMS 季增約 52%、Flash 季增約
35%。毛利率方面，公司平均 53.4%，較前季上揚 11.5ppt，主因受惠記憶體價格
上揚；其中記憶體約 57%；新唐約 39%。簡言之，華邦電 1Q26 營業利益 125.50
億元，稅前淨利 126.88 億元，歸屬母公司稅後淨利 101.14 億元，EPS 2.25 元，
數字優於富邦預期 。(以目前普通股本 450.00 億元計算 ， 未考慮海外可轉債餘額7.50
億美元之稀釋效果；目前轉換價 108.94 元，2027 年 3 月 31 日到期)

記憶體價格幾確定將上漲至年底，2027 年價格走勢尚難以預測 
TrendForce 最新預期 2Q26 DRAM 合約價平均季上揚約 58%~63% ，其中
Consumer DRAM 季上揚約 45~50%；NAND 合約價預估季漲 70-75%。 至於2026
下半年合約價格預期將持續上漲，儘管步調相對溫和，季上揚約3~8%，NAND 漲
幅大於 DRAM。展望 2027 年，記憶體市況轉為 LTA 長約主導，供應商與雲端服務
提供商（CSP）傾向於將 DRAM 與 HBM 協議捆綁銷售，並納入預付款、年度參
考價格與價格上下限等條款 。此外 ，據統計 Google、Amazon、Meta 與 Microsoft 
等四大 CSP 最新的 2026 年資本支出預算再次上修至 7,250 億美元。富邦則看好
CSP 業者投資動作持續，料將支撐記憶體價格在可見未來仍保持高位。

2Q26 售價季增估逾 5 成，EPS 預估升抵 5.54 元 
華邦電 4 月合併營收 192.45 億元，MoM+32.7%，YoY+182.2%；其中記憶體營
收 167.27 億元，MoM+47.2%，YoY+285.3%，月增幅數字優於利基型 DDR4 合
約價之 MoM 幅度 。富邦預估 2Q26 華邦電記憶體均價上揚逾 5 成，主要受惠 DRAM、
SLC NAND 價格漲勢強勁。綜上，我們預估華邦電 2Q26 合併營收約達 592.34 億
元，QoQ+54.8%，YoY+181.8%；毛利率估上升至 66.4%，營業利益估 295.24 億
元，歸屬母公司稅後淨利估 249.37 億元，單季 EPS 估 5.54 元。

富邦不同之處 
富邦目前對華邦電 2026-27 年 EPS 預估值分別為 24.35、37.92 元 ， 相較CMoney
同業預估值分別為+45%、+51%，明顯較為樂觀。

評價與風險 
DRAM 產業上漲趨勢預料將直達 2027 年，華邦電積極擴充產能以捕捉此次景氣上
行的商機。我們重新調整華邦電 FY26-27 獲利預估，以 FY26 之 7.5XPER 或 FY27
之 3.8XPBR 設定目標價為 183.0 元 ， 維持買進的投資評等 。 華邦電的投資風險主要
來自：1)DDR4、DDR3 價格走勢不如預期，2)擴產進度或生產良率低於預期。

面預估仍有個位數以上的上揚，因此預估全年合併營收達 2,501 億元 ，YoY+180%。

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 2026Q1 毛利率受銀等貴金屬成本上升影響降至 34.0%，短期仍需觀察分級漲價與銅製程導
入進度。預估興勤 2027 年營收為 97.3 億元，YoY +8.8%；EPS 16.0 元。2027 年獲利成
長主要來自毛利率回升至 39.0%、產品組合改善與營業槓桿效益。研究部上調目標價 383 元
（24x 27F PER） ，潛在上漲空間 30.9%，投資評等為「買進」 。

+8.3%；毛利率 34.0%，QoQ / YoY 各下滑約 5ppt，
主因銀等貴金屬上漲墊高成本；營業費用率降至約  
14.0%，營業利益率仍維持 19.9%，EPS 2.21 元。

➢ 2026 年成長動能來自 AI 伺服器電源、CDU 與液冷散
熱相關保護元件、車用電子新應用，以及中國太陽能與
儲能需求延續。目前 BB ratio 達 1.5、稼動率接近滿
載，公司預期全年營收呈雙位數成長。

➢ AI 伺服器電源、 CDU 與液冷散熱保護元件已開始交
貨，白金感測器、電流產品與新設計持續送樣。公司去
年訪談曾提及，白金等級保護元件報價可達傳統產品  
10 倍以上，反映 AI 高功率應用對溫度、電流與電壓
保護規格提升；惟歐美大廠由既有供應商轉至台系供應
商認證期較長，實際放量仍需觀察客戶承認進度。

營 運 分 析 
興勤為過電壓、過電流、過溫度三大類保護元件一站式供應商，產品線完整 
興勤電子深耕電路保護元件 47 年，產品涵蓋過電壓、過電流與過溫度三大
類保護需求。過電壓產品包括 MOV 壓敏電阻、TVS 瞬態抑制二極體、ESD 
靜電保護器、GDT 氣體放電管及 MOV+GDT 複合型保護元件；過電流產
品包括 NTC ICL、PTC ICL、PTC 過載保護熱敏電阻及 Polymer PPTC 可
復式保險絲；過溫度產品則包括 NTC 熱敏電阻、PTC 熱敏電阻、家電溫度
感測器、電動車溫度感測器、白金溫度感測器與傳送器，以及溫壓一體感測
器。公司具備完整保護元件產品線，可依客戶不同電路設計需求提供一站式
解決方案。 
公司目前擁有 10 大產品類別、8 個生產據點 、 員工約4,200 人，並於 25 個
國家設有生產與銷售服務據點。 2026Q1 產品應用結構中，資通訊占比  
39.0%，家電與照明  17.0%，工業  16.0%，汽車  16.0%，醫療與其他  
12.0%。保護元件因涉及終端產品安全與可靠度，導入門檻較一般被動元件
高，產品需通過相關安規認證後才能上市。興勤除產品線完整外，亦具備自
有驗證、材料開發、製程設計與應用工程支援能力，有助於切入 AI 伺服器、
車用電子、液冷散熱與高可靠度工業應用。

- 3 - 
個股報告

26Q1 營收年增 8.3%，銀價墊高成本拖累毛利率至 34.0% 
26Q1 合併營收 20.54 億元，QoQ +2.5%、YoY +8.3%，營收端維持成長；
毛利率 34.0%，較 25Q4 與 25Q1 各下滑約 5ppt，主因 2025 年下半年
起銀等貴金屬價格大幅上漲，墊高部分產品成本。營業費用 2.89 億元，QoQ 
-14.0%、YoY -1.8%，費用率降至約 14.0%，較過往 15–16% 區間略有改
善；營業利益率 19.9%。業外由正轉負，拖累稅後淨利降至 2.83 億元，QoQ 
-33.5%、YoY -25.6%，EPS 2.21 元。 
目前為止 2026 年 1–4 月累計營收已達雙位數成長，BB ratio 約 1.5，稼
動率接近滿載。2026 年全年營收成長動能主要來自 AI 伺服器電源、CDU 
與液冷散熱相關保護元件，車用電子由電池模組、充電裝置與馬達延伸至冷
卻系統與 ADAS，中國太陽能與儲能應用持續放量，家電與消費性電子則維
持相對穩定。

AI 伺服器電源、CDU 與液冷散熱應用開始交貨，後續放量按照認證進度 
AI 相關應用為公司近年重要新成長動能。AI 伺服器功耗提高後，電源供應、
UPS、CDU 與液冷散熱系統均需要更高可靠度的電流、電壓與溫度保護元
件。公司表示伺服器電源、CDU 與液冷相關保護應用已於 26Q1 開始交貨，
且白金感測器、電流產品與多項新設計仍持續送樣。由於 AI 電源應用能量
密度提升，對保護元件規格要求同步提高，具備相關技術與認證能力的供應
商相對有限，興勤已具備切入能力。去年訪談曾提到，白金等級保護元件報
價可達傳統產品 10 倍以上，反映高功率 AI 應用對保護元件規格與可靠度
要求提升。由於過去 AI 高規格應用多由歐美供應商供貨，台系供應商切入
需經較長認證期，且各客戶承認進度不同，因此短期不宜過度放大 AI 對營
收的立即貢獻。

車用由三電系統延伸至冷卻系統與 ADAS，2027 後仍為中長期動能車用電
子為公司耕耘多年的中長期市場，產品主要布局電動車三電系統，包括電池
管理模組、電池、驅動馬達與充電相關裝置，近年進一步延伸至冷卻系統與 
ADAS 等新應用。雖然近年整體車用市場成長速度放緩，但車用電子化與電
動化趨勢未變，單車保護元件使用量仍有提升空間。過去公司已切入歐系、
中系與韓系車廠供應鏈，產品定位為零件供應商，主要搭配 Tier 2 客戶，但
終端車廠亦會進行稽核。考量車用產品認證週期較長，已導入之供應關係黏
著度高，可為公司提供穩定營收貢獻。

- 4 - 
個股報告 
圖三、興勤(2428)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐國際預估

分級漲價與銅製程替代並進，第三季後毛利率可望逐步回穩 
2026Q1 毛利率下滑主要來自銀等貴金屬成 本上升。公司目前採取兩項對
策：第一，依市場別、訂單量與成本影響程度，對部分客戶提出分級漲價；
第二，推廣已開發超過 10 年、累計銷售超過 20 億顆且具專利的銅製程產
品，以替代部分貴金屬製程。公司表示，銅製程不僅可降低成本，產品特性
也更穩定，過去已通過市場驗證。 
不過銅製程並非所有產品都能立即替代銀製程，部分應用因終端市場、產品
特性或安規認證要求，仍需使用銀製程或重新通過客戶認證。因此，公司將
依客戶與產品條件分階段導入，無法接受漲價的客戶可評估導入銅製程，而
無法替換製程的產品則需反映成本。

研究部預估興勤 2026年營收 89.4 億元（YoY +11.3%）、毛利率 36.5%、
稅後淨利 16.8 億元（YoY +11.5%）、EPS 13.1 元；2027 年隨高階元件放
量、銅製程滲透率提升與分級漲價效益完整體現，營收 97.3 億元（YoY 
+8.8%）、毛利率回升至 39%（年增 2.5ppt）、稅後淨利 20.4 億元（YoY 
+21.7%）、EPS 16元。公司歷史本益比區間約8-25倍，目前股價對應18.3
倍 27F PER；考量 AI 與車用應用所驅動的需求及毛利回升，本益比評價基
準改採 27F 並上修至 24倍 ， 目標價自182 元調升至 383元（24x 27F PER），
潛在上漲空間 30.9%，投資評等由「逢低買進」調升至「買進」。

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
2436 偉詮電 
(Weltrend Semiconductor Co.)

報告日期： 2026/05/26 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 偉詮電為電源管理與類比 IC 廠，電源、智慧應用  
偉詮電(2436)是發展混合類比、數位線路 IC 設計廠商。偉詮電產
品組合為，1Q26 電源管理 IC 與類比 IC 佔 26%、智慧應用 IC 佔 
43%、電子元件代銷佔 31%，其中智慧應用 IC 成長迅速。產品涵
蓋 MCU、各式數位與類比 IC、擴充至影像處理、影像辨識、高效
能直流無刷馬達等各式演算法以及 8b/32b 泛用型 MCU 等 ， 也代理
ROHM Semi 等品牌之電子零組件。

二. 2026 年 PC/NB 量衰退，在電競、商用與遊戲機進展 
以偉詮電 2025 年產品線 發展，在電源管理 IC 與類比 IC，應用佔
比最高區塊為 NB、遊戲機，NB 市場於 2025 年在庫存方面已漸漸
去化，預期在 Type-C 滲透率提升之下，將有一部分庫存回補之急
單效應。若以 2024~2025 年 Type-C/PD 之市場規模看法，偉詮電在
NB USB Type-C/USB PD 領域於 2024 年滲透率已高達 70%，2025
年滲透率更會達到 80-90%，NB 量增以 AI PC/NB 規格提升之下對
於 USB Type-C/PD 等需求也會提升。偉詮電本身優勢在於電源相
關、DDIC、類比 IC，結合高壓製程、MCU、CPU、韌體製造之整
合能力，在 NB 市場市佔率高於 70%，且在 USB PD、USB 3.1 規格
升級之下受惠。即使 2 0 2 6 年 P C / N B 出貨量預估衰退雙位數
-10%YoY，但偉詮電預估 USB PD 與 Type-C 在電競筆電、商用機
種、遊戲機等新品推展之下 2026 年可望持平或小幅增長。

三.  陞達科技風扇與散熱朝伺服器發展，成為關注焦點 
伺服器散熱風扇的產品線持續整合，偉詮電主要做 MCU，陞達科
主要做 ASIC，公司認為 MCU 的需求會越來越明顯，產品分高階
到低階都會做到 。 伺服器的風扇 ， 主要出貨以 12V 為主 ， 未來 1H26
會發展 48V 產品。USB PD 3.0 過渡到 3.1 或是散熱風扇 IC 由 12V
過渡到 48V ，ASP 可以提高近 2 倍，伺服器散熱風扇的產品發展進
程，成為近期市場關注焦點。NVDA 之液冷 GB200 採用的是 Open 
Loop Liquid Cooling 相當於液體、加上 10 顆~20 顆風扇氣冷(偉詮
電於液冷水泵馬達控制 I C、液冷風扇馬達控制 I C 已經進入
Design-in 階段) ， 公司認為氣冷或 Hybrid 方式還是會是主流 ， 因此 ，
對於散熱風扇控制 IC 市場用於 AI 伺服器的成長看法相對樂觀。
投資建議：區間操作→買進 
目標價位：100.00 元 
      2 2 . 0 0 X  2 0 2 6  E P S

參考價位：80.50 元 
      1 7 . 4 6 X  2 0 2 6  E P S

### Page 2

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

2 
展望 2026 年，NVDA 伺服器搭載新設計，目前 AI 伺服器用散熱
風扇，一台平均搭載 12 顆以上，NVDA 設計為一整機櫃、機櫃旁
SideCar、PowerRack 獨立出來，以 NVDA GB 300 為例，散熱風扇
需要 48V 或 54V 驅動器加上 BBU6L 之後共 40~50 顆風扇(GB 系列
合計需 350 顆)。NVDA Rubin 系列，也採用 54V 驅動器搭載 1~2
座 PowerRack，一座 Rack 需要 70~80 顆散熱風扇(傳統 AC 與 EC 
BLDC 合計) 。偉詮電提及，自身於 AI 伺服器散熱風扇，市佔率至
少 30~50%之間，4Q25 已有一個美系知名 CSP 客戶積極拉貨，未
來 AI 伺服器用散熱風扇會再進軍 ASIC 市場成為下一營運動能。
由於伺服器用散熱風扇所需功能涵蓋逆風齊轉、斷電煞車、電源
與溫度偵測等多項整合型功能，偉詮電在於風扇設計之整合性解
決方案，能夠提供給 CSP 客戶更為完整且性價比高的方案。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 100 元 
偉詮電之產品 AC/DC Switching Regulators、PWM Controllers、USB 
PD Controllers、Fast Charge Prorocol Controllers 等涵蓋 MCU、各式
數位與類比 IC、擴充至影像處理、影像辨識、高效能直流無刷馬
達等各式演算法以及 8b/32b 泛用型 MCU 等，也代理電子零組件。
此外，人型機器人，也需要馬達控制 IC，一人型機器人或倉儲機
器狗需要 20-30 顆馬達控制 IC，機器人關節需 60V 控制 IC 之操作
電壓，屬於高單價產品，目前偉詮電已跟知名學府合作倉儲機器
狗用於智慧工廠運作，機器人的馬達控制 IC 會是下一個成長動
能。展望 2026 年，預估 偉詮電營收為 43.76 億元，+22.61%YoY，
毛利率為 35.93%，稅後淨利為 9.20 億元，稅後 EPS+4.61 元。給予
偉詮電投資建議由區間操作調整至買進，目標價 100 元(22.00x 
2026EPS)。

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介

偉詮電(2436)是發展混合類比、數位線路 IC 設計廠商。偉詮電產
品組合為，1Q26 電源管理 IC 與類比 IC 佔 26%、智慧應用 IC 佔 
43%、電子元件代銷佔 31%，其中智慧應用 IC 成長迅速。產品涵
蓋 MCU、各式數位與類比 IC、擴充至影像處理、影像辨識、高效
能直流無刷馬達等各式演算法以及 8b/32b 泛用型 MCU，也代理電
子零組件等。

What’s Changed
AllRing Tech Co. (6187.TWO) From To
Price Target NT$1,280.00 NT$1,580.00
Computex focus: Rubin/Vera rack designs and thermal/package
updates. We see stronger TSMC CoWoS expansion. We remain

positive on the NVDA supply chain, and raise our PT for AllRing.
Computex runs June 2–5, with the NVIDIA CEO keynote set for June 1. We expect
demos of AI server and networking system designs, not limited to NVIDIA. Key focus:
1. Rubin series sever rack designs: We look forward to the combination of
NVL (GPU rack Kyber with CPO scale-up connection), LPU rack, Vera CPU
rack, BlueField rack, and NVSwitch rack - together targeting the lowest cost
per token for a future AI Factory. We will see if AMD demonstrates Helios

racks sharing token output data.
2. The US$20bn NVIDIA CPU opportunity: Our US semi analyst Joe Moore
expects NVIDIA to provide more details. Our supply chain checks suggest
TSMC is allocating additional CoWoS-R capacity and 3nm wafers for the
Vera CPU. Based on capacity, we think 1.5mn units of standalone CPUs is a
reasonable assumption. End customers could be Microsoft, Meta,
CoreWeave, and Oracle.
3. What about the Rubin schedule and Rubin Ultra package design?

Investors wonder whether Rubin GPU can achieve its target TDP of 2.3kW.
There is also some doubt that Rubin Ultra will be 4-die per package, as
warpage issue still encountered. Our understanding is that Rubin was rolled
back to the original chip thermal solution and achieved 1.8kW TDP; through
server-system thermal collaboration, the 2.3kW TDP can still be achieved.
For Rubin Ultra, NVIDIA needs new photomasks to adopt HBM4e in 2027,
and it should stick with 2-die per package to maintain good chip yield.

We lift our TSMC 2027 CoWoS capacity assumption to 200kwpm from
170kwpm, and slightly trim our SoIC capacity build forecasts: Very strong AI GPU
and CPU demand led TSMC to further expand CoWoS capacity in AP7.  Our checks
suggest TSMC is turning its Fab 15A 28nm/22nm space to 55nm interposer
production. Hence UMC should receive order overflow from Sony’s 28/22nm ISP in
2027; we remain OW on UMC. For SoIC, we still see production unchanged at
30/60kwpm in 2027/2028, while production build might be slightly delayed for

three quarters; we therefore trim our capacity build assumptions to 40/70kwpm for
2027/2028, from 45/78kwpm, respectively. We remain OW on NVIDIA for its key
supply chain for Rubin and Vera, including KYEC, ASE, Hon Precision and  Winway, let
alone TSMC in foundry. We lift AllRing’s price target to NT$1,580, from NT$1,280,
reflecting strong CoWoS capacity build, especially in 2027.

companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Global CoWoS Capacity Update
CoWoS capacity – update on capacity build
Driven by very strong demand coming from AI GPU and CPU and potential rising
competition from other 2.5D packaging solutions, TSMC has decided to further expand its
CoWoS capacity  in AP7 at the expense of SoIC’s capacity expansion, which is less urgent.
We revise our 2027 CoWoS capacity estimate from 160-170kwpm to 200kwpm. TSMC has
confirmed that it is turning its Fab 15A 28nm/22nm space to 55nm interposer production

by adding around 10kwpm, which is further new expansion this year after just building
15kwpm of interposer capacity in Singapore and filling all interposer capacity in Fab 12 and
Fab 14. Hence, we think UMC could receive the order overflow from Sony’s 28nm/22nm
ISP in 2027, and we reiterate OW on UMC.
For SoIC, we still expect production of 30kwpm for 2027 and 60kwpm for 2028. The
production build might be slightly delayed for 3 quarters, and hence we trim our capacity

build assumptions from  45kwpm in 2027 and 78kwpm in 2028 to 40kwpm and 70kwpm,
respectively.

SoIC capacity and demand breakdown
Current customers adopting SoIC include:
• NVIDIA (covered by Joseph Moore): We expect annual SoIC consumption of 6k in
2026, rising to 120k in 2027. This includes demand for CPO's COUPE. We see more
clues from the Asian supply chain that NVIDIA's next-generation GPU Feynman
could require 1-2GB of SRAM stacked on each die within the Feynman package. On
the other hand, we also see NVIDIA studying GPU-to-GPU stacking, which should

translate to SoIC demand.
• AMD (covered by Joseph Moore): AMD has adopted SoIC on its AI and gaming
GPU and high-end CPU, and we  do not rule out future adoption for its ASIC
business. We  expect AMD's SoIC consumption at 42k in 2026, increasing to 60k in
2027.
• Apple (covered by Erik Woodring): Apple's data center and Mac Pro chips rely on
SoIC. We expect Apple's SoIC consumption at 36k in 2026, increasing to 60k in
2027.
• Others: Several customers are now trying TSMC's SoIC capacity, including

Broadcom, Qualcomm (both covered by Joseph Moore), and AWS. We think this is
in line with our understanding that Broadcom would start to introduce SoIC
packaging for its networking chips and ASIC solutions. However, some appear to
still be in the design and trial stage, and not yet finalized.

AllRing: Bottom-up Analysis
We expect CoWoS-related revenue to account for 75% of AllRing's total revenue in 2026,
with AllRing focusing on oS equipment supply to TSMC and ASE/SPIL. However, we see
more non-TSMC 2.5 packaging solution vendors qualifying more Taiwanese CoWoS
equipment vendors, creating a new TAM for AllRing, including FoPLP and Intel's EMIB. We
expect AllRing's 2027 CoWoS revenue to rise 62% Y/Y (TSMC's CoWoS seen at 200kwpm

for 2027), and see it beginning to prepare equipment for CoPoS (if the trial round goes
smoothly in 2026) and its AP fab in Arizona (MP likely starting in 2028).
For CPO, AllRing has penetrated the CPO supply chain of NVIDIA for FAU-optical engine
coupling and FAU AOI equipment. Our latest supply chain checks suggest that AllRing may
have also won the dispensing step after insertion 3, where the optical engine will be
mounted onto the CPO package substrate. We expect more clarity from the company on

CPO-related revenue in 2H26, and expect it to account for 13%, 21%, and 28% of AllRing's
total revenue in 2026, 2027, and 2028, respectively.
For SoIC, we expect 2026 SoIC capacity to reach 14kwpm. TSMC further expanding its
CoWoS capacity in AP7 could cause slightly delays in equipment pull-in. Our latest supply
chain checks suggest that TSMC has revised 2027e capacity to 40kwpm (from 45kwpm)
and 2028e to 70kwpm (from 75kwpm). AllRing is the sole WoW dispenser supplier for

SoIC, but we expect the delay in SoIC to be more than offset by the strong build in
CoWoS. In the long run,  we still  expect it to be the key growth driver for AllRing. SoIC is
one of the key advanced packaging nodes for TSMC in the coming years, as we see more
and more customers adopting this technology to realize chiplet design, including AMD,
NVIDIA, Apple, and Broadcom.

AI semis – stock implications, P/E multiples, revenue 
exposure
• In Asian semis, we are OW on TSMC, KYEC, ASE,   Samsung (covered by Shawn 
Kim), and Aspeed.

AllRing: Valuation Methodology
We lift our RI-based price target (base case scenario value) from NT$1,280 to NT
$1,580: We factor in our estimate changes across 2026-28.  Our other key assumptions are
unchanged, including a cost of equity (CoE) at 8%, derived from a risk-free rate of 2.0%,
beta of 1.0 and market risk premium of 6%, intermediate growth rate of 16% and terminal
growth rate of 4.5%. Our bull and bear case values also rise, from NT$1,800 and NT$520

to NT$2,200 and NT$640, respectively, implying 56x and 16x our 2027 EPS estimate.

Risk Reward - AllRing Tech Co. (6187.TWO)
NT$1,580.00
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions are:Cost of equity (CoE) of 8%, derived from a risk-free rate of 2.0%, beta of 1.0, and a marketrisk premium of 6.0%.Intermediate growth rate of 16%.Cash dividend payout of 65%Terminal growth rate of 4.5%.
MS Rating
▪NVIDIA's next-generation GPU Rubin willstick with graphene for heat sinkattachment, reducing concerns aboutcompetition for Allring.▪We expect AllRing to be a key beneﬁciaryof the multi-year advanced packaging trend,including CoPoS and SoIC, but large-scalerevenue contributions will likely take time.▪It is the sole WoW (Wafer-on-Wafer)dispenser supplier for SoIC, which we expectto be the next growth driver, contributing atleast 2% of total revenue in 2026 and 4% in2027. ▪Our implied 2027e target P/E is 40x,similar to its SoIC peer and lower than CPOpeers. We expect new advanced packagingopportunities including CPO, CoWoP, andCoPoS to beneﬁt the company in the longrun.

NT$2,200.00
56x 2027e EPS
We assume: 1) 60% revenue CAGR over2025-28, thanks to stronger-than-expectedcapacity expansion plans for TSMC's CoWoS,CPO and SoIC; 2) gross margin expands tomore than 55% in 2025-28, due to stronger-than-expected operating leverage resultingfrom the equipment production outsourcingstrategy; 3) new product launches exceedexpectations and AllRing increases itsmarket share in other processes such as"CoW".

NT$1,580.00
40x 2027e EPS
We expect: 1) 44% revenue CAGR in 2025-28e after strong CoWoS capacity expansionfor TSMC in 2024-25; 2) gross margin to bemaintained at 48-53% from 2024-28 vs.48% in 2022 thanks to high operatingleverage resulting from the equipmentproduction outsourcing strategy; and 3) withnew equipment launches, we expect AllRingto expand its market share in otherprocesses. CPO, SoIC and other applicationscontinue to bear fruit.
NT$640.00

16x 2027e EPS
We assume: 1) -30% revenue CAGR in 2024-28, owing to slower-than-expected CoWoScapacity expansion plans by TSMC; 2) grossmargin falls below 40% owing to the highbargaining power of its customers; 3) marketshare loss in "WoS" process amid rising peercompetition.

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Fositek Corp  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
• 2Q26 revenue outlook stays at flat to up 5% QoQ as smartphone hinge
projects entered model transition. Gross margin should thus trend slightly
higher for favorable mix shifting server application.
• 3Q26 revenue outlook should reflect new project rollout benefits for at
least 10% QoQ increase and the growth magnitude depends on new GPU
platform volume ramp progress.
• Fositek should benefit from quick disconnect (QD) supply share gain for new

VR GPU server racks with offerings extended to the compute tray.
• AI ASIC exposure is expanding with new projects in the pipeline from 2H26.
This includes the QD connecting to the cold plate modules for GPU and CPU
as well as optical transceivers (by end of 2026).
• Fositek has implemented automated production lines in China and Vietnam
for total four lines in 1H26.  This should help drive its gross margin higher in
2H26 onward.
• Server rail kit offerings are mainly for general purpose servers now and

Fositek expects further inroads to AI GPU server racks and standalone power
racks in 2H26.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Fositek Corp (6805.TW)
Base case, derived from our multi-stage residual income (RI) valuation model, similar to the
rest of our tech hardware coverage universe. We assume a cost of equity of 10%, a medium-
term growth rate of 14% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Increasing penetration of liquid cooling in servers/data centers
n Faster-than-expected server rail kit project gains
n iPhone flagship models adopt foldable screen in 2026

Risks to Downside
n Slower liquid cooling penetration in servers/data centers
n Slower foldable smartphone penetration
n Stronger-than-expected industry competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Idea
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Taiwan  AI Summit 2026 Key
Takeaways: From Cloud to Edge
MediaTek remains our Top Pick given its strong technology and
execution in Cloud AI ASIC design services. Edge AI also has
strong potential.
What’s new? MediaTek co-COO and CFO Mr. David Ku joined our inaugural AI
Summit to outline the company’s future AI semi strategy. Management views AI as
ubiquitous and plans to pursue a Cloud-to-Edge strategy to capture the opportunity.

We also joined a large-group IR meeting for near-term updates.
Cloud AI:
ASIC business development
• Management reiterated that MediaTek plans to keep adding customer value
and differentiating by technology to avoid ASIC commoditization under the
COT (customer-owned tooling) trend. MediaTek believes Cloud AI ASIC
operating margin can stay above the corporate average.
• Regarding “full system rack support” for Google TPU, management clarified
that MediaTek’s system integration is through chiplet and interconnect

technology, not rack systems or networking design.
• For agentic AI compute, MediaTek plans to participate in PC/server CPU
opportunities through merchant or ASIC design services. The prior NVIDIA
GB10 AI edge server is an example.
• The company doesn’t think major ASIC designs are limited to either training
or inference purposes. We therefore see volume upside for MediaTek’s TPU
v8 (ZebraFish) and v9 (HumuFish) vs. Street consensus which reflects

MediaTek ASIC being used only for AI training.
• After securing the 2nm ASIC (should be TPU v9), the company is bidding for
the next-generation project (which we think should be TPU v10 IceFish; see
our 25 May 2026 report, MediaTek: “Target Price Up” report
). We therefore
see multi-year growth through 2030 (IceFish revenue contribution in 2029
and 2030).
• MediaTek said it has received several CSP RFQs and hopes to add one more
CSP customer by end-2026 (besides Google).

Edge AI: Smartphone and automotive business
• The company said its future growth is “in addition” to smartphone, while
smartphone should continue to grow with edge compute. The company
doesn’t rule out AI service providers reaching out to MediaTek for future AI
phones, given MediaTek’s strong turnkey capability.
• Management sees growth potential in Automotive.
Stay OW, maintain Top Pick: This reflects the company's strong technology and
execution in Cloud AI ASIC design services. Edge AI also has strong potential.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Strong AI ASIC upside in 2028 from 2nm TPU
2nm TPU to adopt dual sourcing – both TSMC CoWoS‑L and Intel EMIB
TSMC’s CoWoS (Chip-on-Wafer-on-Substrate) is the industry’s leading 2.5D packaging
solution, particularly for AI and HPC applications. Its key advantage lies in the use of a
high-density interposer or RDL layer, which enables extremely wide, low-latency
connections between logic dies and HBM stacks. This makes it well suited for bandwidth-

intensive workloads such as GPUs and AI accelerators.
However, these benefits come at a cost: CoWoS – particularly the silicon interposer/bridge
– is expensive, capacity-constrained, and subject to yield challenges, including potential
warpage issues at larger chip sizes. Cost and manufacturing complexity remain key trade-
offs. As a result, we see TSMC’s CoWoS roadmap supporting only up to ~9.7x reticle size,
which does not appear sufficient for future AI or HPC chip designs.

We expect MediaTek to secure a portion of CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure
minimum volume production, but we believe it will use Intel’s (covered by Joseph Moore)
EMIB-T as the primary solution to reduce costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution
proceeds well.
Exhibit 1: TSMC's roadmap for interposer size
Source: Winway, TSMC
Exhibit 2: Intel's EMIB can easily support larger chips  with
more reticles  (>12x) if its supply chain execution proceeds well

Source: Intel
Margin trend and full‑rack system support. In response to our question on providing
full-rack support during its earnings call (referencing recent Broadcom-Google
collaboration), MediaTek indicated it is in discussions with the customer. Management
expects any full‑rack system design support to materialize after 2027.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value addition to the customer
across both silicon and packaging design. As a result, ASIC should remain accretive to

operating margins

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Google 2nm TPU the major growth driver for MediaTek from
2028
On April 22, Google introduced TPU v8 at Google Cloud Next, featuring two purpose-built
architectures for training and inference: TPU 8t and TPU 8i. Our recent supply chain
checks across multiple companies on reticle size confirm that MediaTek’s 3nm TPU 8
corresponds to TPU 8t. This supports our view that MediaTek’s TPU performance should
be sufficient for AI training.

According to Google’s announcement, “both chips will be generally available later this
year and can be used as part of Google’s AI Hypercompute,” in line with MediaTek’s
guidance that AI ASIC will contribute ~US$2bn of revenue in 2026 (we now assume 450-
500k units at a US$4,500 ASP).
In addition, our recent checks suggest that ASP for the next-generation 2nm Humufish
TPU, supported by MediaTek, could exceed our prior expectations, driven by an increased

number of main dies. For Humufish, checks across multiple sources indicate the number of
main dies per chip could significantly exceed Zebrafish (one compute die with one I/O die).
Combined with a SerDes upgrade and larger packaging size, we estimate ASP at US$12-15k
(we now model US$13k).
At the gross margin level, management indicated margins vary by project, which may also
reflect potential full-rack system support. We model 2nm TPU gross margin at c.35% (vs.

c.40% for 3nm TPU) given larger revenue scale, while we still expect operating margin at
~20-25%, above the smartphone BU’s 15-20%.

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
Android AI agent phones as an emerging trend from Google
I/O
Android AI agent phones – a trend to watch: Google is actively rolling out AI agents on
Android phones, starting with Galaxy S26 and Pixel 10 in early 2026. These agents can
perform multi-step tasks across apps – for example, ordering food, organizing notes, or
creating calendar events through direct interaction with app UIs. Over time, we expect
broader adoption across the Android ecosystem. Token consumption and cost remain key

constraints for agentic AI, and we believe on-device computing on edge devices such as
smartphones will help alleviate part of this cost burden.
Google I/O 2026 – Gemini evolves into an Agentic platform: At Google I/O 2026,
Google repositioned Gemini from a chatbot to a proactive AI “personal agent” capable of
autonomously handling tasks across apps and services. Google introduced several new
technologies, including Gemini Spark – a persistent assistant operating in the background

across Workspace tools – Gemini 3.5 Flash for faster real-time interaction, and Gemini
Omni for advanced multimodal capabilities spanning text, audio, images, and video. The
company also announced AI-driven changes to Google Search and deeper Gemini
integration across Android, YouTube, shopping, and future XR devices, signaling a broader
push to embed AI as a continuously available assistant that manages workflows and
automates user tasks. We believe the agentic AI replacement cycle could start in 2027

from high-end Android phones – another option value on MediaTek.
Exhibit 7: Gemini transforms into 'agentic' platform
Source: Google

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside
n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.

n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Quanta Computer Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026
Takeaways
• NB made up <15% of the revenue mix in 1Q, and will remain below 20% for
the full year.
• AI server capacity will double by end-2026 vs. end-2025, primarily in
Thailand and the US. Hence, Quanta's capex guidance is NT$30bn.
• VR200 rack shipments should be limited in CY26, with some sampling
shipments expected to start in 3Q26. The majority of shipments will happen
in CY27.

• Some customers are willing to accept the consignment model to alleviate
ODMs' dual burdens of c apex expansion and working capital pressures,
thereby preventing potential supply constraints and roadmap delays from
longer investment cycles (owing to lack of funds).
• NVIDIA is still looking to standardize the computing trays for Rubin, but
Quanta is not sure if the CSP customers will accept. NVIDIA might run the
risk of losing more share to the ASIC camp if it insists on standardizing the

again in CY26.
General server sales guidance: up in double digits y/y in CY26 (unchanged vs. 1Q
earnings call), but units to grow y/y as well.

Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), an 8.5% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Stronger-than-expected server demand
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Softer-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Weak margin performance owing to rising labor costs and sales shortfalls
n Fierce price competition in the mega data center segment
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

WT Microelectronics Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
Outlook by segments: Data center and communication  segments remain the
primary growth driver with strong revenue visibility into 2H. Industrial demonstrates
a structural recovery with projected ~20% Y/Y growth for 2026, driven by power
component demand. Auto segment shows recovery momentum in Europe and
Americas, while the Asian markets remains sluggish, especially China market, and

overall moderate growth is expected in 2Q. Consumer/PC segment demand visibility
is low following customer pull-in in 2H25, Mobile phone segment is still expected to
experience seasonal uplifts in 2H26. 2Q financials likely to fall at the mid-point of
guidance (
link).
Future Electronics currently accounts for around 10% revenue contribution due to
WT's strong data center growth. Currently, Future's ROWC is below corporate
average.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
WT Microelectronics Co. Ltd. (3036.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We assume a cost of equity of 12.6% (2% risk-free rate, 11.8%
risk premium, 0.9 beta), a medium-term growth rate of 10% and a long-term growth rate of
3%.
Risks to Upside
n Margin trends up even amid low-margin AI business.
n Global semi inventory drops, reflecting strong mobile device demand.
n Market share expands.

Risks to Downside
n Margin trends down dramatically amid slower-than-expected AI-semi development.
n Global semi inventory rises, reflecting poor mobile/PC device demand.
n Market share lost.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

We attended MediaTek’s pre-Computex event on 29 May, where 
management walked through the company’s positioning across 
cloud and edge under the “One MediaTek” framework. The central 
narrative was MediaTek’s transformation “from edge to cloud”: 
building on a dominant edge/device franchise (#1 in smartphone 
SoC and in smart-home connectivity), it is now carrying that 
compute and connectivity IP up into the data center, while 
agentic AI simultaneously pushes more intelligence back down 
to all edge devices. Management now see its TAM expanding from a 
US$60-70bn TAM in 2020 towards US$200bn+ as Generative AI 
becomes the key semiconductor demand driver. 
Management also reiterated US$2bn data center/AI ASIC revenue 
in 2026 and a US$70-80bn 2027 ASIC TAM with a 10-15% market 
share target — the same figur
 es it had raised guidance to at its 
1Q26 analyst meeting on 30 April. The value of this event, in our 
view, was the deeper technical showcase of MediaTek’s full-stack 
custom-ASIC capability (SerDes, XPU, packaging, memory, 
interconnect) and the clear articulation of its ecosystem role via 
various collaborations such as NVLink Fusion and Microsoft 
micro-LED AOC. Please see below for our takeaways. 
See also: MediaTek (2454.TW) The AI ASIC upcycle is just getting started;

### Page 3

share target first raised at the 1Q26 analyst meeting. Management sees such rapid
growth in TAM being supported by the a ccelerating capex from Top 4 Hyperscalers, and
MediaTek has won multiple large programs with very strong pipelines of new
programs coming in, driving its confidenc e in capturing the 10-15% of market share in
a short period of time. Overall, management positioned MediaTek not as a newcomer
but as a custom-silicon vendor since 2015: having started in networking ASIC, it has

progressively built strong capabilities in developing complex multi-die solutions, which
now underpin its full-stack solutions.
Full-stack custom ASIC capability, not just an XPU design house
Management highlighted that MediaTek can supply the entire stack required for a
competitive AI a ccelerators. On SerDes, in which MediaTek is best-in-class, its 224Gbps
solution is now available across multiple process nodes and is now ready for production.
On XPU, MediaTek works on both training and inf erence accelerators, integrating

high-speed SerDes and optical/electrical interfaces beyond just logic and HBM, and is
working on designs of more than 10x reticle size today. On packaging, MediaTek
supports TSMC CoWoS and is one of very few vendors to also support EMIB,
positioning itself as a “one-stop shop” while also exploring Co-Packaged Optics
(CPO). On interconnect, it spans on-package die-to-die (UCIe, Mlink), on-board
chip-to-chip (PCIe, micro-LED), and off-boar
d (Ethernet, CPO solutions). In our view, the

breadth of MediaTek’s product suites reduces customer’s integration risk and support
both project wins and dollar content growth per design.
NVLink Fusion and Microsoft AOC partnership extend the ecosystem positioning
MediaTek showcased two industry partnerships (Nvidia/Microsoft) during the event. For
Nvidia’s NVLink Fusion, a customer can develop custom silicon with MediaTek, which
provides an ASIC template for the XPU and integrate a chip-to-chip interface that allows

the design to move beyond the ASIC tray into the Nvidia NV switch tray, paving the way
for scalable rack-to-rack solutions and then use Infiniband or Ethernet t
o scale out the
rest of the datacenters solution. This integration enables faster-time-to-market for
custom silicon that operate with Nvidia rack-level infrastructure. Separately,
management also introduced an active optical cable (AOC) solution that adopts
micro-LED, which is co-developed with Microsoft. Management claims that the AOC

solution enjoys 50% power reduction vs. VCSEL-based solution, while offering the
reliability of copper with much further reach. Lastly, management clarified tha t
MediaTek is not launching its own rack-level solution; rather it provides adjacent
solutions, such as retimers, AOC, NVLink Fusion, to shortern time-to-market for
customers beyond the largest four CSPs.
Compute: winning the “agentic PC era”
Management claimed that the compute business has grown +80% YoY and just crossed

US$1bn in revenue, spanning tablets, Chromebooks, and AI workstations. Management
also flagg ed strong interest in the Nvidia DGX Spark, supported by robust generative and
agentic AI workload. Another key highlight is that MediaTek’s market share in
Chromebook CPU has risen significantly fr
om 8% in 2023 to 30% in 2025, and
expected to reach 50%+ in 2026. Management has positioned Kompanio (MediaTek’s
SoC solution for Chromebook) as one of the most-powerful mainstream CPU with

longest battery life, which is expected to best supports Google’s Gemini model going
29 May 2026   3
Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

forward.
Automotive: Dimensity AX and the shift to AI-defined vehicles
Management detailed the Dimensity AX platform across cockpit, connectivity, and
components. The cockpit platform pairs a MediaTek compute die with an Nvidia GPU die
on CoWoS packaging, supporting Nvidia CUDA ecosystem, while the connect platform
covers NR-NTN satellite connectivity, 5G-A, WiFi 8, and dual-Bluetooth technologies. On
traction, management cited 20+ OEM partnerships and 35m+ cars already using

MediaTek solution, with the business up 385% over the past fiv
e years.
The bigger strategic message was the industry’s shift from software-defined v ehicles
(SDV) to AI-defined v ehicles (AIDV) - vehicles whose features are enabled by AI and
whose tasks are carried out by intelligent agents running rapidly evolving models.
Management characterized an AIDV by four traits: 1) multi-model experiences that
sense beyond voice input to take in sight, sounds, emotion, health and the surrounding

environment, 2) proactive, always-on services that anticipate the user’s need, 3)
concurrent execution of complex tasks across multiple users and scenarios than one at a
time, and 4) hybrid edge-to-cloud compute rather than purely local processing. To
deliver this, MediaTek’s Dimensity AX platform provides powerful, concurrent
multi-model inference at up to 400 TOPS, with AI-bandwidth meaningfully reduced via
compression and a multi-core GPU. Net net, MediaTek positioned the Dimensity AX

family as purpose-built to support the transition toward AI -defined v
ehicles.
Edge: device leadership as the anchor, AI glasses as the next platform
Management reinforced MediaTek’s edge breadth as both a profit anchor and an
on-ramp for agentic AI. It has been the number one smartphone SoC vendor for more
than fiv e years, with growth driven by the premium and flagship segment, and its next
flagship mo ving to TSMC N2 in 3Q26. In connectivity, it continues to extend its lead from

WiFi 6 through 7, with WiFi 8 expected to be introduced in 2028, and it also claimed to
have the number one global market share in smart-home devices. The clearest new
edge platform is AI glasses, which management framed as a major opportunity
growing from around 10mn units worldwide in 2025 towards 100mn units a year in
2030, and as a natural extension of its edge franchise as agentic AI brings more
intelligence to all devices. Here MediaTek leans on its 3D low-power computing IP , and

the main challenges it flagg ed are battery life, a form factor small enough for stylish
designs, and proactive AI that can sense the surrounding environment. Taken together
with its leadership in smartphones, connectivity and the smart home, AI glasses are
becoming the next platform that will keep MediaTek at the center of the edge as agentic
AI proliferates.

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology
Investment Thesis - Mediatek
MediaTek is a leading global IC design house specializing in smartphone AP (application
processor). We have a positive stance on MediaTek, viewing it as well-positioned to
transition from a traditional smartphone application provider to an AI-focused vendor,
beginning with AI smartphones and extending to enterprise ASICs and smart automotive
solutions (in partnership with NVIDIA) in 2025 and beyond. We expect MediaTek to

achieve solid multi-year growth, where we expect revenue and earnings to increase by
40%/57% CAGRs, respectively, in 2025-28E. This growth will be primarily driven by: 1)
market share gains, particularly in the premium segment (specifically high-end 5G
flagship SoCs), 2) s
trong ramp in AI ASIC business, and 3) new TAM in
automotive/computing sectors.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Asia   AI Summit 2026 Feedback
Silergy AI Summit takeaways:
• 2026 full-year guidance: Management expect 20%+ Y/Y revenue growth in
2026, driven by data center and auto. 2H Y/Y growth is expected to
accelerate vs. 1H.
• Margin outlook: GM declined in 1Q on inventory impairment, lower auto
mix, and higher foundry costs. Management expect 1Q26 margins to mark
the yearly bottom.
• End-market mix: Consumer 36%, automotive 13%, communication 6%,

computer 14%, industrial 33% in 1Q26.
• Data center products:
Enterprise SSD, optical module, and general server
motherboards. Data center products span industrial and compute, ~15% of
mix in 1Q26; management expect ~20% by year-end 2026.
• 8-inch/12-inch mix:
In 2025, >90% of wafers were 8-inch. By year-end 2026,
>20% will be 12-inch.
• Auto semi localization in China: The company benefits from industry
consolidation and expanding global market share of Chinese auto makers.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Silergy Corp. (6415.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We assume a cost of equity of 9.5% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6%

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

revenue.
Hardware status: 50G VCSEL short-distance solutions are in mass production, and

100G variations are fully verified. Laser diode focus remains on EML and CW laser

2027 or beyond.
LEO satellite demand:
LEO satellite requires expanded Power Amplifier arrays.
Transmission hardware is transitioning toward higher frequency E-band, V-band, and
W-band arrays.
WiFi7 projections: Generational migration is expanding from initial flagship
smartphone down to high-end and mid-range devices.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Date: 2026-06-01

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on Delta after our 2026 Taiwan 
Corporate Day. We reaffirm our positive view on Delta and view it as one of 
our top ideas within the AI trend.

What’s the impact: Position in the power segment remains resilient. 
Based on our understanding, we believe there are four potential power 
architecture solutions to be applied in the Vera Rubin generation, with two 
from a high-voltage environment and the other two from a low-voltage 
environment. The high-voltage environment could be divided into 800VDC 
or +-400VDC, while the total voltage from rack could range from 600kW to 
1.5MW, depending on client requirements. As for low-voltage options, these 
can either be 72kW powershelf (bundled with 6x of 12kW PSUs) or 110kW 
powershelf (bundled with 6x of Nvidia’s standard 18.3kW solution), but 
width of 18.3kW power supply will be 3x bigger than Delta’s 12kW solution. 
We assume Nvidia’s strategy to use HVDC has sped up by c.1 year, and it 
would have 20-30% of HVDC adoption from this generation and 50% from 
2027. This is the key driver of our higher AI server revenue assumption.  
New growth drivers anticipated by investors. During Taiwan Corporate 
Day, we believe investor interest has gradually shifted to fuel cells (ie, 
SOFC or SOEC). Due to the technology licenses from Ceres Power (CWR 
L), Delta plans to build up trial fuel cell-capacity from 4Q26 and start mass 
production in 2027. We believe investors are keen to know the competition 
between Ceres and Bloom Energy in terms of tech spec and Delta’s 
competitors (Weichai [2338 HK, HKD40.14, Outperform (2); covered by 
Kelvin Lau] and Doosan Enerbility [034020 KS, KRW106,900, Buy (1); 
covered by Mike Oh]), which are also licensed from Ceres Power. We 
believe it will become the next growth driver for Delta after HVDC plays out. 
The micro grid (vs. utility grid) concept with rising new-type energy 
generation method (eg, fuel cells), solid-state transformers (SSTs), Energy 
Storage System (ESS) could enable Delta to further transform itself from a 
power systems company into an infrastructure provider.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 8-9% to reflect 
better-than-expected gross margin performance and AI server contributions 
on rapid adoption of HVDC from Nvidia. We raise our 12M TP to TWD2,922 
(from TWD2,440), based on a higher target PER of 55x (from 50x; above 
its past-3-year range of 11-50x) applied to our 1-year-forward EPS, due to 
new growth drivers from the micro grid. We believe a higher target PER is 
justified as the PEG ratio stands at 0.90x (vs. our 2025-28E earnings 
CAGR of 61%). Downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 4-36% above Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish HVDC adoption rate.

Company profile 
Founded in 1971, Delta Electronics has been the global leader in switching power supply solutions since 
2002 and DC brushless fans since 2006. Its business covers 3 key areas: power electronics (eg, power 
supplies, thermal management products and passive components), automation (eg, controllers, robot 
arms and building management systems), and infrastructure (eg, datacentre infrastructure, networking 
and EV charging).

ESG analysis

Update Date: 1 Jun 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESGrisks
Risks ManagementAnalystcomments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Delta'sboardhas7non-independentand5independentdirectorswithatermofthreeyears.
Thetotalnumberofdirectorsandratioofindependentdirectorsappearhigherthanmostother
listedcompanies.Besides,accordingtoDelta'sCSRreport,enhancingtheboard'sfunctionsis
oneofthekeygoalsforthecompanytoachievebettercorporategovernance;also,theboard
members'diversityandprofessionalismwillbetakenintoconsiderationtoensureagoodboard
quality.Moreover,thecompanyevaluatesallthedirectorsintheboardusinginternalregulations
andanexternalindependentinstitutioneachyearandatleastonceeverythreeyears,
respectively.WebelieveDeltahasimplementedrelativelymoreenhancedmeasurethanmost
listedfirmstoimproveitsboardquality.

Profitability turnaround in April
Turning profitable in April: Powerchip reported Apr 2026 net profit of NT$552mn,
+243.46% Y/Y; April 2026 EPS of NT$0.13, +244.44% Y/Y. Apr 2026 net margin was
10.94%. This is not comparable to 1Q26 net margin, which was skewed by NT
$20.1bn gains from the Fab P5 disposal and NT$5.5bn related impairments booked in
1Q. Excluding these gains and impairments, 1Q26 net margin would be -2.7%.

Therefore,  April's profit reflects the company actually turning profitable excluding
one-off items. The 10.94% margin is also stronger than our 2Q26e net margin
estimate of 7.1%. April EPS of NT$0.13 represents 66% of our 2Q26e EPS estimate
of NT$0.29.

benefit from the mature mode up-cycle, and the volume ramp-up of the company's

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co (6770.TW)
Base case, 2.1x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with our approach for Greater China memory
IDM players, in view of the industry's high volatility. Our 2.1x target P/B is higher than the
historical average of 1.5x since 2021, reflecting our optimistic view on improved pricing and
utilization.
Risks to Upside
n Pricing power is sustained in 2026.

n Listing on main board attracts investment fund flows.
n Aluminum process enjoys higher margins.
Risks to Downside
n Competition from China intensifies.
n Price erosion is faster than expected.
n Demand from consumer electronics clients weakens further.
n Inventory in the supply chain takes longer to digest.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

陳映慈 
Lucy.YT.Chen@yuanta.com 
陳冠成 
charleschen24@yuanta.com

1Q26 客/貨運量年增 6.6%/7.6%，EPS 年增 24%，優於預期 
1Q26 營收 570.0 億元(季增 5.2%/年增 8.0%)，春節旅遊旺季，載客率年增
5.1 個百分點、客運運量年增 6.6%，貨運包機需求暢旺，運量年增 7.6%；
毛利率年增 2.4 個百分點至 20.6%，航空燃油耗用價格季減 4%/年減 6%，
且票價因需求暢旺上漲， 營業利益 64.4 億元(季增 26.9%/年增 13.9%)，
EPS 0.85 元(季增 39.6%/年增 23.5%)，優於我們/市場預期 18%/34%。 
2Q26 載客率高、運價大漲，但燃油成本大增估 EPS -0.06 元 
上修 2Q26 營收 18.2%至 615.6 億元(季增 8.0%/年增 19.9%)，估客運營收
季減 6%/年增 9%，區域航線需求保持強勁，且中東航班供給減少推升歐洲
航線需求；估貨運營收季增 42%/年增 42%，美伊戰事推升貨運價格，4 月
單位收益月增 21.1%/年增 15.9%；預估毛利率年減 14.5 個百分點，新加坡
燃油價格 3 月至今均價上漲至每桶 184 美元(vs 2 月均價 89 美元)，2Q 用油
成本季增超過 100%，估營業虧損 5.3 億元，EPS -0.06 元(季轉虧/年轉虧)。 
高價燃油衝擊獲利，估 2026 年 EPS 年減 23%，維持持有 
預估 2026 年營收年增 13.0%，上修 11.4%，基於 1)客運營收年增 10.4%，
受惠經濟表現強勁、股市創高， 旅遊及商務需求保持強勁，且中東運能受限
帶動轉乘需求外溢， 1Q26 歐洲線客貨運營收年增 26%；2)貨運營收年增
20.1%，中東戰事大幅推升空運價格，國籍航空自 5/1 起再調漲貨運燃油附
加費近 100%，且 AI 出貨強勁、荷姆茲海峽封鎖使高時效貨物轉空運，上修
運價展望；但因燃油價格及費用高於預期，下修營業利益率 0.4 個百分點，
估營業利益年減 29.5%、EPS 年減 23.2%至 1.82 元。儘管客貨運展望佳使
營收上修，但高油價成本將使獲利下滑，即便衝突平息，仍預期因能源供應
瓶頸使高油價維持數月，對華航看法中性， 評價維持 1.3 倍目標本淨比、
2026 年預估每股淨值 17.0 元，推得目標價 21 元，維持持有評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
1Q26 春節旺季、出口暢旺，客/貨運運量年增 6.6%/7.6%，EPS 優於
預期；5 月貨運燃油附加費調漲近 100%，估 2Q26 營收年增 19.9%。 
⧫  
上修 2026 年營收 11.4%，反映中東運能受限推升歐洲航線需求、貨運
運價大漲，但 2Q 起將反映高價燃油，估 EPS 年減 23.2%至 1.82 元。 
⧫  
燃油價格高檔壓抑獲利，票價轉嫁有限，評價維持 1.3 倍 2026 年每股
淨值 17.0 元，目標價 21 元，維持持有評等。

營運分析  
1Q26 客/貨運量年增 6.6%/7.6%，EPS 年增 23.5%，優於預期 18% 
華航 1Q26 營收 570.0 億元(季增 5.2%/年增 8.0%)，優於我們/市場預期 6%/4%，其中客運營收季
增 13%/年增 9%至 357 億元，優於預期，春節及寒假旺季、日本棒球經典賽及賞櫻熱潮挹注、地
緣政治緊張提升歐美航線需求，載客率年增 5.1 個百分點，運量(RPK)季增 3.5%/年增 6.6%、單位
收季增 7.4%/年增 0.6%；貨運營收季減 9%/年增 5%至 165 億元，雖為淡季，但 AI 伺服器、半導
體持續出貨、包機需求踴躍， 貨運運量(FTK)季減 5.4%/年增 7.6%、但單位收益季減 3.7%/年減
5.1%，主因 2025 年同期關稅政策帶動提前備貨需求，推升運價 ；1Q26 毛利率季增 2.5 個/年增
2.4 個百分點至 20.6%，受惠航空燃油耗用價格(12 至 2 月平均)季減 4%/年減 6%，且票價因需求
暢旺上漲，營業利益 64.4 億元(季增 26.9%/年增 13.9%)、稅後淨利 51.7 億元(季增 39.6%/年增
23.5%)，EPS 0.85 元，優於我們/市場預期 18%/34%。

2Q26 歐洲線受惠轉乘效益、貨運運價大漲，但燃油成本大增，估 EPS -0.06 元 
華航 4 月營收 208.5 億元(年增 16.5%)，達成我們前次/市場 2Q26 營收預期 40%/40%，優於預期，
考量貨運運價上漲、客運需求暢旺，上修 2Q26 營收 18.2%至 615.6 億元(季增 8.0%/年增 19.9%)，
客運部分，估營收 335 億元(季減 6.0%/年增 8.7%)，清明連假、復活節、賞櫻旺季及泰國潑水節等
節慶需求強勁，且中東航班供給減少推升歐美航線需求，4 月運量/單位收益年增 1.2%/5.4%；貨運
部分，估營收 233 億元(季增 41.8%/年增 42.2%)，主因美伊戰事推升空運運價，4 月貨運單位收益
月增 21.1%/年增 15.9%；預估毛利率年減 14.5 個百分點至 6.1%，下修 0.2 個百分點，主因新加
坡燃油價格 3 月至今均價上漲至每桶 184 美元(vs 2 月均價 89 美元)，4 月起將開始反應高價燃油，
2Q26 整季用油成本對比 1Q26 成長超過 100%，估營業虧損 5.3 億元(季轉虧/年轉虧)、稅後淨損
3.9 億元(季轉虧/年轉虧)，EPS -0.06 元。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：航空公司 2026 年 5 月 29 日 China Airlines 第4 頁，共 18頁  
燃油價格飆漲使成本壓力攀升，票價轉嫁有限 
華航一年用油桶數約 1,600 萬桶，1Q26 燃油成本佔整體成本 30%，假設票價不變、未進行避險、
美金兌台幣匯率 31.5 元，航空燃油價格全年均價上漲 10 美元，將使華航成本增加 50 億元，使
EPS 減少 0.83 元。最新 5 月航空燃油均價回落至 136 美元，但對比 2025 年均價 86 美元仍處高價
位，即便衝突平息，仍預期因能源供應瓶頸使高油價將維持數月，而公司已透過調漲燃油附加費以
反映成本，貨運方面因國內 AI 出貨強勁，運價明顯上漲，但在客運票價方面，在市場競爭及需求
彈性考量下，成本轉嫁給消費者空間有限，預估整體獲利將自 2Q26 起面臨成本端壓力而下滑。 
上修 2026 年營收反映客貨運價量齊揚，但燃油價格仍在高檔，估 EPS 年減 23% 
預估華 航 2026 年 營收 年 增 13.0%至 2,363 億元 ，上修 11.4%，基 於 1) 預估客 運營 收年 增
10.4%(前次預估年增 1.5%)，受惠經濟表現強勁、股市創高，國人旅遊及商務需求保持強勁，且中
東運能受限帶動轉乘需求外溢，1Q26 歐洲線客貨運營收年增 26%；2)預估貨運營收年增 20.1%(前
次預估年增 0.4%)，主因中東戰事大幅推升空運價格，國籍航空自 5/1 起再調漲貨運燃油附加費，
台灣出口至歐/美地區自每公斤 41 元上漲至 81 元、出口至亞洲地區自 14 元上漲至 28 元，且國內
AI 出貨強勁、荷姆茲海峽封鎖使高時效貨物轉空運，上修後續運價展望；預估 2026 年毛利率年減
3.5 個百分點至 14.3%，主因中東戰事導致燃油價格上漲，本次假設全年燃油均價每 桶 120 美元，
較前次 100 美元上修，估營業利益年減 29.5%至 146.8 億元、稅後淨利年減 23.2%至 110.8 億元，
估 EPS 1.82 元，上修 11.8%，主因上修客運載客率及貨運運價展望，抵銷高油價對毛利率之負面
影響。 
華航機隊持續汰舊換新以提升燃油效率，2026 年機隊預計淨成長 7 架 
截至 2026 年 3 月，華航擁有機隊共 86 架，包括 68 架客機與 18 架貨機。公司預計 2026 年底前客
機總架數增加 7 架至 75 架(圖 7)，廣體客機包括 5 架 787-9 及 2 架 A350-900，將優化長程航線運
能結構及航網彈性，窄體客機有 6 架 A321neo，將接替目前的 737-800 成為區域線的主力客機，
預計到 2028 年 737-800 將全數退役。 
貨機方面，2026 年規劃將以 10 架 777F 及 8 架 744F 彈性派飛長短航線，為國籍航空唯一具有雙
機種貨運航機優勢之航空公司， 777F 主要特色為節油效能佳，派飛長程航線節油效能顯著、 744F
具備對特殊大型、可開鼻頭裝載貨物之酬載優勢，在區域線集貨及特殊貨物裝載可凸顯其特性，雙
機種兼顧大運量及省油優勢，未來將逐年汰換 744F，並新增 4 架 777F 貨機和 8 架 777-8F 貨機。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：航空公司 2026 年 5 月 29 日 China Airlines 第6 頁，共 18頁  
產業概況  
IATA 樂觀看待 2026 年國際航空客貨運市況，總收入續創新高 
根據 IATA 統計，2025 年全球航空產業總收入年增 4.1%至 1.008 兆美元，2026 年預估將年增
4.5%至 1.053 兆美元，儘管面臨供應鏈瓶頸、地緣政治衝突及貿易環境改變，但客貨運需求持續
強勁，客運票價及貨運運價亦保持穩定，帶動產業持續成長。 
客運市場-預估 2026 年全球旅客人次年增 4.4%，亞太地區持續為全球客運主要成長動能 
2025 年全球客運旅客年增 4.3%至 49.8 億人次、收入客公里(RPK)年增 5.2%、客運單位收益年減
0.9%，帶動收入年增 4.2%至 7,160 億美元，較 2019 年成長約 13%，而全年總運能(ASK)年增 
4.9%，整體載客率為 83.7%，為歷年最高，反映供應鏈問題限制運能增長問題加劇。展望 2026 年，
IATA 預測全球航空旅客人次年增 4.4%至 52.0 億人次、RPK/ASK 年增 4.9%/4.7%、客運單位收益
年減 0.1%，帶動客運收入年增 4.8%至 7,510 億美元，其中亞太地區客運量成長率達 7.3%居全球
之冠，顯示亞太仍為全球客運主要成長動能，成長來自於中國和印度旅遊活動增加及中產階級擴張，
同時中國逐步放寬旅遊限制。長期而言，全球經濟發展支撐客運需求維持正向成長， Boeing/Airbus
分別預測全球航空客運量 2025-2043 年 CAGR 達 4.2%/3.6%、IATA 預期全球客運市場 2024-2043
年 CAGR 達 3.8%，至 2043 年成長至 79 億人次，亞太地區 2024-2043 年 CAGR 則達 5.3%。 
貨運市場-時效性貨物及電商包裹需求強，預估 2026 年全球貨運量年增 2.4% 
2025 年全球貨運量僅 年增 0.2%至 6,990 萬噸、單位收益年減 0.5%，使全球貨運市場營收年增
2.6%至 1,550 億美元，主因貿易保護措施抑制需求，如美國 2025/8/29 起取消所有國家小額包裹關
稅優惠，而紅海危機緩和、空運海運價差逐步回升抑制海轉空效應，但仍顯著高於  2019 年的 
1,010 億美元，顯示航空貨運已轉向高值、時效性貨物。展望 2026 年，IATA 預估 2026 年全球貨
運量將年增 2.4%至 7,160 萬噸、CTK 年增 2.6%、單位收益年減 0.5%，帶動貨運收入年增 2.1%
至 1,580 億美元，受惠於時效性貨物及電商包裹需求持續成長。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：航空公司 2026 年 5 月 29 日 China Airlines 第7 頁，共 18頁  
2025 年全台出入境人次回復至疫情前 96%，2026 年有望追平歷史高峰 
2019 年全台出入境人數達 7,182 萬人，創歷史新高，其中桃園機場出入境人數約 4,869 萬人(佔
68%)，後續因疫情爆發，各國施行出入境管制、邊境封鎖，2021 年全台出入境人次跌落至 760 萬
人次，隨後經歷解封、運能逐漸回升，2025 年受惠台灣新增 4+1 天假期、台幣升值等利多，全台/
桃機出入境人數年增 8%/6%至 6,868 萬人/4,780 萬人，回到疫情前 96%/98%水準。展望 2026 年，
民航局亦預測國際線運量年增 3%至 6,200 萬人次，創歷史新高。 
根據民航局統計，2025 年冬季班表國際線飛航 107 個航點，航班數已攀升至每週平均 2,981 班，
相較前期夏季班表每週多出 124 班，並超越 2019 年疫情前的每週 2,957 班其中又以中東恢復比例
最多達 214％，北美恢復 140％次之，日韓則恢復 125％，港澳與中國航線僅分別恢復至疫情前的
89%與 51%。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：航空公司 2026 年 5 月 29 日 China Airlines 第8 頁，共 18頁  
2026 年轉機動能續強，國籍航空將持續強化北美航網 
台灣位處亞太地區中心，桃園機場在中轉便利性方面維持競爭優勢， 2025 年不含過境之轉機人數
達 670 萬人次，占整體運量 14%，較疫情前成長 25.7%，其中往來東南亞與北美經桃園機場轉機
旅客人數持續創高。 
以亞太機場往北美航點轉運占比來看，桃園機場占比達 22.1%，僅次於仁川 23.6%，並領先東京羽
田 21.6%、香港 17%與上海浦東 3%，顯示桃機在長程航線轉乘效率與區位銜接性方面具備相對競
爭力。轉機至北美的旅客占桃園機場轉機人次約 4 成，我國國籍航空持續強化北美布局，包含長榮
航空將於 2024/10 開航桃園-達拉斯、星宇航空於 2025/6 與 2026/1 開航安大略、鳳凰城、華航預
計於 2025/12 開航鳳凰城，我們認為隨台商赴美投資設廠後將帶來的龐大商務需求，轉機客將為
2026 年客運市場一大成長動能，而國籍航空公司亦將持續開闢新航點以搶攻北美市場。  
圖 14：2019-2025 年桃機出入境/轉機人次 圖 15：經桃機之東南亞往返北美人數持續增加

2025 年桃機總貨量年增 10.1%，高價值產品支撐空運需求 
台灣貨運市場進口/出口/轉口分別佔 25%/25%/50%，其中轉口及出口運量與美國 PMI 走勢及台灣 
PMI 新出口訂單呈正向，進口則根據國內需求成長，故與台灣 GDP 正相關。2H20-2021 年因海運
塞港貨物海轉空，貨運運量年增 20.0%、創下歷史新高，2022 年初烏俄戰爭、美國 3 月開始升息，
導致全球性消費減弱而製造業庫存去化周期延長，加上客機逐漸回歸提升載貨運能，空運供過於求，
2022/23 年台灣總貨運量年減 9.7%/16.8%。 
2024/25 年桃機總貨量年增 7.5%/10.1%至 250 萬噸，超越疫前水準，尤其出口貨量成長 25.2%最
為突出，顯示台灣在全球供應鏈重組下受惠，同時 AI 等新興科技及跨境電商需求強勁。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：航空公司 2026 年 5 月 29 日 China Airlines 第9 頁，共 18頁  
航太供應鏈持續不順，新機交遞延限制運力增長幅度 
由於人力短缺、供應鏈斷裂、品質控管問題、罷工事件頻傳、軍用規格插單等因素， 兩大飛機製造
商 Airbus/ Boeing 尚有 8,977/6,726 架民航機訂單未交付，主要來自 737 系列與 A321neo 機型供
應短缺，對比 2025 年整體交機數約 1400 架、1Q26 各自交機量分別為 114/143 架，推算現有積
壓訂單仍需 8-10 年才有機會消化完畢，全球航空市場供給新增速度仍大幅受限於生產能量。在民
航機供應受限情況下，2025/26 航空公司運能(ASK)年增 4.9%/4.2%，新增供給成長率將低於過往
平均之 7~9%，有助支撐票價與提升載客率。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：航空公司 2026 年 5 月 29 日 China Airlines 第10 頁，共 18頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
上修 2026 年營收反映客貨運價量齊揚，但燃油價格仍在高檔，維持持有評等 
展望 2026 年，上修營收 11.4%至 2,363 億元，基於 1)預估客運營收年增 10.2%(前次預估年增
1.5%)，國人旅遊及商務需求保持強勁 ，且中東運能受限帶動轉乘需求外溢； 2)預估貨運營收年增
20.1%(前次預估年增 0.4%)，主因中東戰事大幅推升空運價格，且國內 AI 出貨強勁、荷姆茲海峽
封鎖使高時效貨物轉空運，上修後續運價展望；下修毛利率 0.1 個百分點至 14.3%，中東戰事導致
燃油價格上漲，本次假設全年燃油均價每桶 120 美元，較前次 100 美元上修；下修營業利益率 0.4
個百分點，主因人事費用高於預期，1Q26 費用年增 33.7%；估營業利益年減 29.5%至 146.8 億元、
稅後淨利年減 23.2%至 110.8 億元，估 EPS 1.82 元，上修 11.8%，主因上修客運載客率及貨運運
價展望，抵銷高油價對毛利率之負面影響。展望 2027 年，同步上修客貨運需求，但下修營業利益
率 0.8 個百分點，反映費用高於預期。 
華航股價目前交易於 2026 年預估每股淨值 1.1 倍本淨比，歷史 3 年本淨比區間為 1.1-2.2 倍(平均
1.5 倍)，儘管客運需求暢旺及貨運運價大漲帶動營收上修，但考量 2Q26 起將開始反映高油價成本
使獲利下滑，對華航看法中性，評價維持 1.3 倍目標本淨比、2026 年預估每股淨值 17.0 元，推得
目標價 21 元，維持持有評等。

公司簡介  
華航成立於 1959 年，提供國際客貨運運輸服務，其中以客運為主。客運以亞洲線為主、貨運則以越太平洋為主，截至
3M26，機隊共 86 架航機，其中客機 68 架、貨機 18 架。華航 1-4M26 於台灣航空市場客運/貨運市佔率達 19%/35%，分
別為台灣第二/一大業者。此外，華航旗下子公司台灣虎航，為台灣唯一低成本航空公司，華航持股約 69%，1Q26 貢獻華
航營收/稅後淨利約 55/13 億元，2026 年底機隊規模將達 19 架，以日本、韓國為主要營運航線。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 華航整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行於略為領先的位置，
在航空行業中的公司中亦排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 華航的整體風險曝險為中等，略低於行業平均。產品治理、碳排放（自有營運）和人力資本是
值得關注的重要 ESG 議題。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：華航在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬上等。該公司在多個關鍵的 ESG
（環境、社會和治理）領域表現優異。公司擁有強而有力的環境政策，體現了其對生態責任的明確承諾。其 ESG 報告
體系完善，符合國際最佳實務標準。公司對 ESG 議題的監督機制健全，並設有專門的董事會或執行委員會。然而，其
舉報人保護機制雖然基本上到位，但在覆蓋範圍和保護力度方面仍有提升空間。

分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明： (1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或
證券發行機構之看法；(2) 研究部分析師於本研究報告中所提出之特定投資建議或觀點，與其過去、現在、未來薪酬的任何部份皆無直接或間接關聯。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

台勝科(3532) 
買進, TP 380 (潛在報酬 31.03%)

Target Price：380

台勝科 1Q26 營運已呈現需求回升跡象，12 吋矽晶圓受惠記憶體
需求增強、8 吋則受 AI 伺服器相關應用帶動，出貨量皆較上一季
成長， 惟晶圓代工成熟製程仍處庫存調整階段 ，加上新廠折舊攤提
壓力，使短期毛利率與獲利表現仍明顯承壓。展望 2026 年，隨客
戶庫存策略由去化轉向回 補，短、中、長期訂單能見度改善， AI
應用推升記憶體、先進製程及成熟製程需求，帶動 8 吋與 12 吋出
貨動能逐步回升； 同時公司已針對下半年價格進行合理調整， 若價
格調整效益逐步發酵 ， 將有助於緩解折舊與原物料成本壓力。 中長
期來看，2027 至 2028 年全球主要半導體廠商將進入近年最大擴
產週期，12 吋麥寮新廠產能已被客戶預約完售，且公司傾向維持
LTA 比例彈性、保留較多產能於現貨市場，若現貨價格如預期上
行，並搭配先進封裝、HBM、interposer、carrier wafer 及 3D
封裝特殊矽晶圓等高階應用開發推進， 將有助於提升營收彈性、 改
善獲利結構並強化高階矽晶圓競爭力。 綜合以上， 研究部預估台勝
科 2027 營業收入為 224.44 億元，Y+47.91%、毛利率 27.18%，
Y+16.48ppts、 營業利益率23.55%，Y+16.71ppts、 稅後純益44.74
億元，Y+327.07%、EPS 為 11.54 元。台勝科歷史本益比區間為
10~35 倍 ， 研究部考量整體產業已看到現貨價上漲趨勢 ， 且公司將
不傾向提高 LTA 比例，下半年議價有上調獲利空間機會，且新廠
產能已全數完售，給予上緣評價 33 倍，目標價為 380 元，投資建
議為買進。

 會社 、 台灣塑膠工業股份有限公司及亞太投資股份有限公司共同出
資成立， 2006 年日本小松電子金屬將股權轉讓予日本 SUMCO
株式會社，並於 2007 年更名為台塑勝高科技，公司於 2006 年登
錄興櫃，並於 2007 年轉上市。台勝科為 8 吋及 12 吋矽晶圓製造
商，產品包含拋光晶圓、熱處理晶圓及磊晶晶圓。產品主要應用於 
IC、DRAM、光電二極體、分離式元件、晶圓代工及太陽能電池用
基板等領域，客戶群則以晶圓代工與 DRAM 製造產業為主。總部
與主要生產基地位於雲林縣麥寮鄉台塑工業園區 ， 擁有一座8 吋及
一座 12 吋矽晶圓製造廠，為因應客戶製程微縮與品質要求提升，
台勝科於 2015 年在日本成立久原拉晶工廠，以 因應台塑集團內
DRAM 矽晶圓原料供應能力；並於 2021 年宣布於雲林麥寮擴建
12 吋矽晶圓廠。前十大股東持股合計86.60%。主要競爭者為日本
信越 Shin Etsu、合晶(6182.TT)、環球晶(6488.TT)等。

⚫ 產業概況 
AI 與記憶體需求推升，產業將進入上升循環 
目前全球矽晶圓市場需求可大致分為兩大類：一是受 AI、HPC 及
先進製程節點帶動的高階應用需求，二是消費性電子、工業與車用
等成熟製程需求。現階段 AI、HPC、雲端運算與資料中心擴建需
求仍維持強勁，使 12 吋先進製程用晶圓需求保持高成長動能，部
分供應商 12 吋晶圓產能已呈現滿載狀態。相較之下，消費性電子
市場表現仍偏保守 ， 但成熟製程相關的工業應用與車用需求已逐步
出現復甦跡象，帶動 8 吋晶圓需求回溫。 
整體而言，矽晶圓市場目前仍處於約5%至 10%的供給過剩階段，
但隨著 AI、先進製程、EPI 及 HBM 用拋光晶圓需求持續擴大，產
業供需結構有望逐步改善，並帶動矽晶圓產業重啟上升週期。價格
方面，目前市場已觀察到現貨價與合約價回升趨勢，晶圓供應商預
